当期野鸟数量加倍与预期哺乳动物数量增加 57.9% 相关(95% CI 43.5-73.8%),上个月野鸟数量加倍与预期哺乳动物数量增加 15.9% 相关(95% CI 5.2-27.8%)。在为期 6 个月的时间坚持中,尽管总检测量仍然被高估,但增加相同州野生鸟类的暴露使平均绝对误差降低了 23.8%,均方根误差降低了 27.9%。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,野鸟 监测 记录包含有关哺乳动物 HPAI 检测活动的短期、地理本地信息。然而,协会并没有建立直接的传播,并且需要在业务预测之前进行进一步校准。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过强调野生鸟类检测数据对哺乳动物 HPAI 活动的潜在预测价值,为 监测 策略提供信息。这可能有助于风险评估和有针对性的监测工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据受到 监测 记录的使用以及总检测结果可能过度预测的限制。由于这些限制和进一步校准的需要,研究结果的可信度中等。

Short-term associations between wild-bird and mammalian high pathogenic avian influenza detection in the United States

Authors: H. Fatoyinbo
Original abstract (English)
High pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) detections in mammals have raised concern about repeated wildlife spillover and the targeting of mammalian surveillance. We evaluated whether wild-bird detection records contained short-term explanatory and predictive information about mammalian detections in the United States. Public surveillance records from April 2022 to July 2026 comprised 18,523 wild-bird and 815 mammalian detections. Monthly state-level mammalian counts were analysed using negative-binomial mixed models adjusted for seasonality, preceding mammalian detections and state-level heterogeneity. The selected model contained contemporaneous and one-month-lagged same-state wild-bird counts. A doubling of one plus the contemporaneous wild-bird count was associated with a 57.9% increase in the expected mammalian count (95% CI 43.5-73.8%), and a doubling of the preceding-month count with a 15.9% increase (5.2-27.8%). Bordering-state exposure did not improve fit. In a six-month temporal holdout, adding same-state wild-bird exposure reduced mean absolute error by 23.8% and root mean squared error by 27.9%, although total detections remained overpredicted. After exact-row deduplication, the corresponding effects were 59.7% and 13.3%. Wild-bird surveillance records contained short-term, geographically local information about mammalian HPAI detection activity, but the associations do not establish direct transmission and require further calibration before operational forecasting.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address concerns about repeated wildlife spillover of high pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) and the need for mammalian surveillance. The research sought to evaluate whether wild-bird detection records could provide short-term explanatory and predictive information about mammalian HPAI detections in the United States.
Design and population
The study analyzed public surveillance records from April 2022 to July 2026, which included 18,523 wild-bird and 815 mammalian detections. The focus was on state-level mammalian counts and their association with wild-bird detection data.
Core methods
Monthly state-level mammalian counts were analyzed using negative-binomial mixed models, which accounted for seasonality, preceding mammalian detections, and state-level heterogeneity. The model incorporated contemporaneous and one-month-lagged same-state wild-bird counts.
Main results
A doubling of the contemporaneous wild-bird count was associated with a 57.9% increase in the expected mammalian count (95% CI 43.5-73.8%), and a doubling of the preceding-month wild-bird count was associated with a 15.9% increase (95% CI 5.2-27.8%). In a six-month temporal holdout, adding same-state wild-bird exposure reduced mean absolute error by 23.8% and root mean squared error by 27.9%, although total detections remained overpredicted.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that wild-bird surveillance records contain short-term, geographically local information about mammalian HPAI detection activity. However, the associations do not establish direct transmission and require further calibration before operational forecasting.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform surveillance strategies by highlighting the potential predictive value of wild-bird detection data for mammalian HPAI activity. This may aid in risk assessment and targeted monitoring efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by the use of surveillance records and the potential for overprediction of total detections. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to these limitations and the need for further calibration.

禽类和人类甲型流感病毒通过粘液凝胶网络的体外渗透

Penetration of avian and human influenza A viruses through mucus gel networks in vitro
作者： Maximilian Busch, Fiona Riegel, Ege Duran, Cosmin Stefan Butnarasu, Daniel Christian Lauster, Christian Sieben, Maximilian Rohde
摘要中文翻译摘要 甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 由于其连续的 进化 和人畜共患的 传播 仍然是一个公共卫生问题。呼吸道粘液是吸入病毒遇到的第一个细胞外屏障,但研究流感病毒通过粘液的传播和转移病毒群体的感染性的标准化方法仍然有限。在这里,我们建立了体外病毒-粘液渗透测定来分析 IAV 与标准化含粘蛋白基质的相互作用。我们使用在气液界面条件下培养的人 Calu-3 气道上皮细胞的粘液和牛颌下粘蛋白作为替代基质。通过总蛋白对制剂进行标准化,并使用生化和生物物理方法进行表征。我们将人类季节性 H1N1pdm 病毒与高致病性禽 H5N1 病毒进行了比较。通过 RT-qPCR 对病毒 基因组 转移进行定量,同时评估易感细胞中保留的感染性。与对照溶液相比,两种含有粘蛋白的基质都限制了病毒的通过。病毒 RNA 转移和保留感染性因基质和病毒而异,但在任一基质内均未检测到 H1N1pdm 和 H5N1 之间存在统计学上的显着差异。这些结果共同建立了一个框架,将粘液表征与病毒传播和感染性的补充测量相结合。该方法为在受控条件下研究流感病毒-粘液相互作用提供了基础,并且可以扩展到其他 IAV 毒株和生理相关的粘液来源。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 因其连续的 进化 和人畜共患的 传播 仍然是一个公共卫生问题。本研究旨在调查 IAV 通过呼吸道粘液的传播情况以及转移病毒群体的感染性。
设计与对象
建立了体外病毒-粘液渗透试验来分析 IAV 与标准化含粘蛋白基质的相互作用。该研究使用人 Calu-3 气道上皮细胞的粘液和牛颌下粘蛋白作为替代基质。它将人类季节性 H1N1pdm 病毒与高致病性禽 H5N1 病毒进行了比较。
核心方法
我们建立了体外病毒-粘液渗透试验来分析 IAV 与标准化含粘蛋白基质的相互作用。通过总蛋白对制剂进行标准化,并使用生化和生物物理方法进行表征。通过 RT-qPCR 对病毒 基因组 转移进行定量,同时评估易感细胞中保留的感染性。
主要结果
与对照溶液相比,两种含有粘蛋白的基质都限制了病毒的通过。病毒 RNA 转移和保留感染性因基质和病毒而异,但在任一基质内均未检测到 H1N1pdm 和 H5N1 之间存在统计学上的显着差异。
解释与创新
这些结果建立了一个将粘液特征与病毒转移和感染性的补充测量相结合的框架。该方法为在受控条件下研究流感病毒-粘液相互作用提供了基础,并且可以扩展到其他 IAV 毒株和生理相关的粘液来源。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更有效的 监测 和流感病毒诊断方法提供信息,特别是在了解它们与呼吸道粘液的相互作用方面。这可能有助于设计有针对性的干预措施来预防病毒 传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外条件,并未考虑体内因素。证据是温和的,因为它为未来的研究提供了框架,但没有提供关于现实世界中病毒行为的明确结论。

Penetration of avian and human influenza A viruses through mucus gel networks in vitro

Authors: Maximilian Busch, Fiona Riegel, Ege Duran, Cosmin Stefan Butnarasu, Daniel Christian Lauster, Christian Sieben, Maximilian Rohde
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Influenza A viruses (IAVs) remain a public-health concern because of their continuous evolution and zoonotic transmission. Respiratory mucus is the first extracellular barrier encountered by inhaled viruses, yet standardized approaches for studying influenza virus passage through mucus and the infectivity of transferred virus populations remain limited. Here, we established an in vitro virus–mucus penetration assay to analyze IAV interactions with standardized mucin-containing matrices. We used mucus from human Calu-3 airway epithelial cells cultured under air–liquid interface conditions and bovine submaxillary mucin as surrogate matrices. Preparations were normalized by total protein and characterized using biochemical and biophysical methods. We compared a human seasonal H1N1pdm virus with a highly-pathogenic avian H5N1 virus. Viral genome transfer was quantified by RT-qPCR, while retained infectivity was assessed in susceptible cells. Both mucin-containing matrices restricted viral passage compared with control solutions. Viral RNA transfer and retained infectivity varied by matrix and virus, although no statistically significant difference between H1N1pdm and H5N1 was detected within either matrix. Together, these results establish a framework integrating mucus characterization with complementary measurements of viral transfer and infectivity. This approach provides a basis for investigating influenza virus–mucus interactions under controlled conditions and can be extended to other IAV strains and physiologically relevant mucus sources.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A viruses (IAVs) remain a public-health concern because of their continuous evolution and zoonotic transmission. This study aimed to investigate the passage of IAVs through respiratory mucus and the infectivity of transferred virus populations.
Design and population
An in vitro virus–mucus penetration assay was established to analyze IAV interactions with standardized mucin-containing matrices. The study used mucus from human Calu-3 airway epithelial cells and bovine submaxillary mucin as surrogate matrices. It compared a human seasonal H1N1pdm virus with a highly-pathogenic avian H5N1 virus.
Core methods
We established an in vitro virus–mucus penetration assay to analyze IAV interactions with standardized mucin-containing matrices. Preparations were normalized by total protein and characterized using biochemical and biophysical methods. Viral genome transfer was quantified by RT-qPCR, while retained infectivity was assessed in susceptible cells.
Main results
Both mucin-containing matrices restricted viral passage compared with control solutions. Viral RNA transfer and retained infectivity varied by matrix and virus, although no statistically significant difference between H1N1pdm and H5N1 was detected within either matrix.
Interpretation and novelty
These results establish a framework integrating mucus characterization with complementary measurements of viral transfer and infectivity. This approach provides a basis for investigating influenza virus–mucus interactions under controlled conditions and can be extended to other IAV strains and physiologically relevant mucus sources.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective surveillance and diagnostic methods for influenza viruses, particularly in understanding their interaction with respiratory mucus. This could aid in the design of targeted interventions to prevent viral transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro conditions and does not account for in vivo factors. The evidence is moderate as it provides a framework for future research but does not offer definitive conclusions on viral behavior in real-world settings.

在柬埔寨活禽市场进行部分盖子干预期间,对空气和表面病毒信号以及工人可接受性进行宏基因组评估

Metagenomic assessment of airborne and surface viral signals and worker acceptability during a partial-lid intervention in a Cambodian live bird market
作者： Frida E. Sparaciari, Peter Cronin, Vireak Heang, Songha Tok, Sarath Sin, Chenthearath Rith, Limmey Khun, Sithun Nuon, Chakriyouth Top, Ly Ley
摘要中文翻译摘要 机械脱毛可以分散活禽市场中的病毒物质,但介入性机器改造必须适合日常工作。这项非随机实地研究评估了柬埔寨奥鲁西市场脱毛机的部分盖子,为期六个采样日,其中三个有盖子,三个没有盖子。靶向 RT-qPCR 和目标富集宏基因组学对三小时空气采样期间收集的空气样本以及该时间之前和之后收集的附近表面样本中的病毒 RNA 进行了表征,同时工作问卷评估了可用性。测序检测到 73 个家禽相关病毒类群。在 18 个空气样本中的 14 个中,甲型流感病毒、禽冠状病毒和轮状病毒 G 合计占相对宏基因组丰度的 80% 以上。在空气样本中,RT-qPCR 循环阈值与 禽流感 病毒和新城疫病毒的宏基因组相对丰度呈负相关(Spearman ρ 分别 = -0.645 和 -0.684)。在戴盖子的日子里,空气中的丰富度明显较低,但目标病毒检测并没有一致变化,而且成分分布重叠。在有盖和无盖的日子里,空气采样期之前和之后的表面轮廓发生了变化;该研究并未确定这些变化与眼睑相关的差异。所有五名工人均表示感到不适并认为工作速度较慢;没有人愿意使用经过测试的盖子,但如果将其永久集成到机器中,则有三个人会继续使用。由于每种情况仅采样三天,该研究并未确定与盖子使用相关的空气传播或表面病毒污染的持续减少。未来的研究应该评估与工人一起重新设计的盖子,以及解决通风、卫生、清洁水和废物管理的补充措施,以减少职业接触。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估活禽市场中部分盖子修改对空气传播和表面病毒信号的影响,解决在机械拔毛过程中减少病毒传播的需要,同时保持工人的可接受性。
设计与对象
在柬埔寨奥鲁西市场进行了一项非随机实地研究,为期六个采样日,其中三天在脱毛机上部分盖上盖子,三天没有盖上盖子。在三小时内收集空气和表面样本,并使用工人问卷来评估可用性。
核心方法
使用靶向 RT-qPCR 和目标富集宏基因组学来表征空气和表面样本中的病毒 RNA。空气样本是在三小时的空气采样期内收集的,而表面样本是在此期间之前和之后收集的。工作调查问卷评估了员工的可接受性和感知的工作流程变化。
主要结果
测序检测到 73 个家禽相关病毒类群。在 18 个空气样本中的 14 个中,甲型流感病毒、禽冠状病毒和 G 轮状病毒合计占相对宏基因组丰度的 80% 以上。在戴盖子的日子里,空气中的丰富度明显较低,但目标病毒检测并没有一致变化,而且成分分布重叠。
解释与创新
作者解释说,部分眼睑干预可能降低了空气传播的病毒丰富度,但目标病毒检测缺乏一致的变化表明需要进一步研究来证实其有效性。这项研究为机器改造对活禽市场病毒传播的潜在影响提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,改造带有部分盖子的脱毛机可能是减少活禽市场中空气传播病毒信号的潜在策略,这可能为未来改善职业安全和减少人畜共患 传播 风险的干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究每种情况只有三天的采样时间,这限制了持续减少与盖子使用相关的空气或表面病毒污染的能力。由于样本量有限和研究结果的描述性,证据强度中等。

Metagenomic assessment of airborne and surface viral signals and worker acceptability during a partial-lid intervention in a Cambodian live bird market

Authors: Frida E. Sparaciari, Peter Cronin, Vireak Heang, Songha Tok, Sarath Sin, Chenthearath Rith, Limmey Khun, Sithun Nuon, Chakriyouth Top, Ly Ley
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Mechanical defeathering can disperse viral material in live poultry markets, but interventional machine modifications must fit routine work. This nonrandomized field study evaluated partial lids on defeathering machines at Orussey Market, Cambodia, across six sampling days, three with lids and three without. Targeted RT-qPCR and target-enriched metagenomics characterised viral RNA in air samples collected during a three-hour air-sampling period and in nearby surface samples collected before and after that period, while work questionnaires assessed usability. Sequencing detected 73 poultry-associated viral taxa. Influenza A virus, avian coronavirus and rotavirus G together accounted for over 80% of relative metagenomic abundance in 14 of 18 air samples. In air samples, RT-qPCR cycle thresholds correlated inversely with metagenomic relative abundance for avian influenza virus and Newcastle disease virus (Spearman’s ρ = −0.645 and − 0.684, respectively). Airborne richness was descriptively lower on days with lids, but targeted viral detections did not change consistently, and composition profiles overlapped. Surface profiles changed from before to after the air-sampling period on days with and without lids; the study did not establish a lid-associated difference in these changes. All five workers reported discomfort and perceived slower work; none wanted to use the lid in its tested form, although three would continue if it were permanently integrated into the machine. With only three sampling days per condition, the study did not establish a consistent reduction in airborne or surface viral contamination associated with lid use. Future studies should evaluate lids redesigned with workers alongside complementary measures addressing ventilation, hygiene, and clean water and waste management to reduce occupational exposure.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the impact of partial-lid modifications on airborne and surface viral signals in live bird markets, addressing the need to reduce viral dispersion during mechanical defeathering while maintaining worker acceptability.
Design and population
A nonrandomized field study was conducted at Orussey Market, Cambodia, over six sampling days, with three days featuring partial lids on defeathering machines and three days without. Air and surface samples were collected during a three-hour period, and worker questionnaires were used to evaluate usability.
Core methods
Targeted RT-qPCR and target-enriched metagenomics were used to characterise viral RNA in air and surface samples. Air samples were collected during a three-hour air-sampling period, while surface samples were collected before and after this period. Work questionnaires assessed worker acceptability and perceived changes in workflow.
Main results
Sequencing detected 73 poultry-associated viral taxa. Influenza A virus, avian coronavirus, and rotavirus G together accounted for over 80% of relative metagenomic abundance in 14 of 18 air samples. Airborne richness was descriptively lower on days with lids, but targeted viral detections did not change consistently, and composition profiles overlapped.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the partial-lid intervention may have reduced airborne viral richness, but the lack of consistent changes in targeted viral detections suggests that further studies are needed to confirm its effectiveness. This study provides novel insights into the potential impact of machine modifications on viral dispersion in live bird markets.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that modifying defeathering machines with partial lids could be a potential strategy for reducing airborne viral signals in live bird markets, which may inform future interventions to improve occupational safety and reduce zoonotic transmission risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study had only three sampling days per condition, which limits the ability to establish a consistent reduction in airborne or surface viral contamination associated with lid use. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limited sample size and the descriptive nature of the findings.

美国加州中央山谷越冬沙丘鹤 (Antigone canadensis) 中甲型流感抗体的有限检测

Limited Detection of Influenza A Antibodies in Sandhill Cranes (Antigone canadensis) Wintering in the California Central Valley, USA
作者： Jeffery D. Sullivan, Rebecca L. Poulson, Michael L. Casazza, Elliott L. Matchett, Cory T. Overton, Deborah L. Carter, Alisha D Bundy, Mike Carpenter, Jacob W Chappell, Greg Golet
摘要中文翻译进化枝 2.3.4.4b 高致病性 H5N1 的出现导致全球鹤种群中出现前所未有的 死亡率 事件。尽管人们越来越关注,但人们对甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 的 患病率 以及沙丘鹤 (Antigone canadensis) 中产生的抗体知之甚少。为了弥补这一知识差距,我们对 2023-2024 年和 2024-2025 年季节在加利福尼亚州中央谷越冬的 31 只大沙丘鹤和两只小沙丘鹤(分别是 Antigone canadensis tabida 和 Antigone canadensis canadensis)进行了采样,以评估 IAV 的活跃感染情况。通过 rRT-qPCR 检测,所有采样个体均未检测出 IAV 感染呈阳性。我们还对 29 只大沙丘鹤和两只小沙丘鹤进行了采样,以获取之前接触过 IAV 的血清学证据;通过 bELISA,四只大沙丘鹤的 IAV 抗体检测呈阳性,其中两只个体中检测到了 H5 特异性抗体。这些发现表明,之前曾接触过,可能是在繁殖地,那里有与高致病性 H5 相关的鹤死亡记录。然而,本研究中观察到的低 血清流行率 表明,加利福尼亚州斯塔滕岛的越冬种群可能仍然在很大程度上容易受到未来 暴发 的影响,尽管小样本量确实限制了这些发现的更广泛的适用性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
进化枝 2.3.4.4b 高致病性 H5N1 的出现导致全球鹤种群中出现前所未有的 死亡率 事件。该研究旨在调查甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 的 患病率 以及沙丘鹤 (Antigone canadensis) 中产生的抗体。
设计与对象
为了弥补这一知识差距,我们对 2023-2024 年和 2024-2025 年季节在加利福尼亚州中央谷越冬的 31 只大沙丘鹤和两只小沙丘鹤(分别是 Antigone canadensis tabida 和 Antigone canadensis canadensis)进行了采样,以评估 IAV 的活跃感染情况。
核心方法
我们还对 29 只大沙丘鹤和两只小沙丘鹤进行了采样,以获取之前接触过 IAV 的血清学证据;通过 bELISA,四只大沙丘鹤的 IAV 抗体检测呈阳性,其中两只个体中检测到了 H5 特异性抗体。
主要结果
通过 rRT-qPCR 检测,所有采样个体均未检测出 IAV 感染呈阳性。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,之前曾接触过,可能是在繁殖地,那里有与高致病性 H5 相关的鹤死亡记录。
科研与公卫意义
本研究中观察到的低 血清流行率 表明,加利福尼亚州史泰登岛的越冬种群可能仍然在很大程度上容易受到未来 血清流行率 的影响,尽管小样本量确实限制了这些发现的更广泛的适用性。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告与研究设计或样本量相关的任何具体限制。

Limited Detection of Influenza A Antibodies in Sandhill Cranes (Antigone canadensis) Wintering in the California Central Valley, USA

Authors: Jeffery D. Sullivan, Rebecca L. Poulson, Michael L. Casazza, Elliott L. Matchett, Cory T. Overton, Deborah L. Carter, Alisha D Bundy, Mike Carpenter, Jacob W Chappell, Greg Golet
Original abstract (English)
The emergence of clade 2.3.4.4b highly pathogenic H5N1 has led to unprecedented mortality events in crane populations globally. Despite increasing concern, little is known about the prevalence of influenza A virus (IAV) and resulting antibodies in Sandhill Cranes (Antigone canadensis). To address this knowledge gap, we sampled 31 Greater and two Lesser Sandhill Cranes (Antigone canadensis tabida and Antigone canadensis canadensis, respectively) wintering in California’s Central Valley during the 2023–2024 and 2024–2025 seasons to assess active infection with IAV. None of the sampled individuals tested positive for infection with IAV via rRT-qPCR. We also sampled 29 Greater and two Lesser Sandhill Cranes for serological evidence of prior exposure to IAV; four Greater Sandhill Cranes tested positive for IAV antibodies via bELISA and antibodies specific to H5 were detected in two individuals. These findings suggest prior exposure, potentially on breeding grounds where crane mortalities related to highly pathogenic H5 have been documented. However, the low seroprevalence observed in this study suggests that the Staten Island, California, wintering population may remain largely vulnerable to future outbreaks, though small sample size does limit the broader applicability of these findings.

Question and background
The emergence of clade 2.3.4.4b highly pathogenic H5N1 has led to unprecedented mortality events in crane populations globally. The study aimed to investigate the prevalence of influenza A virus (IAV) and resulting antibodies in Sandhill Cranes (Antigone canadensis).
Design and population

Core methods

BSP3 是一种法氏囊源性四肽,可在体外促进禽 B 淋巴细胞增殖并增强小鼠的抗原特异性抗体反应

BSP3, a bursal-derived tetrapeptide, promotes avian B lymphocyte proliferation in vitro and enhances antigen-specific antibody responses in mice
作者： Xiaohan Ma, Xiaodong Liu
摘要中文翻译法氏囊是鸟类独特的中枢免疫器官,源自该器官的生物活性肽在免疫调节中发挥着重要作用。然而,新型、结构简单的肽的鉴定及其潜在的分子机制仍有待充分阐明。本研究从白羽肉鸡法氏囊中分离得到一种新型四肽,命名为BSP3(法氏囊源小肽3;Leu-Leu-Pro-Gly,LLPG),分子量为398.50(m/z),并通过高效液相色谱(HPLC)和基质辅助激光解吸/电离飞行时间串联质谱法进行鉴定。(MALDI-TOF-MS/MS);BLASTP分析证实了其序列的独特性。小鼠体内实验表明,BSP3和灭活的H9亚型禽流感病毒疫苗联合免疫显着增强血清抗原特异性IgG反应,促进IFN-γ和IL-4分泌,并增加抗原刺激的脾淋巴细胞增殖。在体外,BSP3在5μg/mL的最佳浓度下促进鸡B淋巴细胞系DT40的增殖。对 BSP3 处理的 DT40 细胞进行探索性转录组分析,鉴定出 1,100 个表达发生改变的候选基因,这些基因被注释为 RNA 加工、肌动蛋白交联形成和细胞外基质组织;MAPK 信号传导位于通路相互作用网络的核心。RT-qPCR 验证与转录组数据一致。总之,BSP3 是一种新型的法氏囊衍生四肽,在小鼠和禽类 B 细胞中显示出免疫调节活性。在 BSP3 被视为候选 疫苗 佐剂之前,需要在禽类 宿主 中进行进一步验证和功能保护研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索源自鸟类法氏囊的生物活性肽的免疫调节潜力,因为已知这些肽在免疫调节中发挥重要作用。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用鸡 B 淋巴细胞系 DT40 的体外实验和小鼠体内实验,其中 BSP3 与灭活的 H9 亚型 禽流感 病毒 疫苗 联合免疫。
核心方法
使用高效液相色谱 (HPLC) 和基质辅助激光解吸/电离飞行时间串联质谱 (MALDI-TOF-MS/MS) 从白羽肉鸡的法氏囊中分离出 BSP3。BLASTP分析证实了其序列的独特性。在体外,测试了其最佳浓度5 μg/mL对B淋巴细胞增殖的影响。在体内,通过与灭活的 H9 亚型 禽流感 病毒 疫苗 联合免疫来评估其对抗原特异性抗体反应的影响。
主要结果
体内实验表明,BSP3和灭活的H9亚型禽流感病毒疫苗联合免疫显着增强血清抗原特异性IgG反应,促进IFN-γ和IL-4分泌,并增加抗原刺激的脾淋巴细胞增殖。
解释与创新
作者将 BSP3 解释为一种新型法氏囊衍生四肽,对小鼠和禽类 B 细胞均具有免疫调节活性,突出了其作为候选 疫苗 佐剂的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,BSP3 可能是增强 疫苗 对 禽流感 病毒功效的有前途的候选者,可能改善鸟类和哺乳动物 宿主 的免疫反应。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠体外和体内实验,需要在禽类 宿主 中进行进一步验证,以确认其作为 疫苗 佐剂的潜力。由于研究结果的实验​​性质,证据是中等的。

BSP3, a bursal-derived tetrapeptide, promotes avian B lymphocyte proliferation in vitro and enhances antigen-specific antibody responses in mice

Authors: Xiaohan Ma, Xiaodong Liu
Original abstract (English)
The bursa of Fabricius is a unique central immune organ in avian species, and bioactive peptides derived from this organ play important roles in immunomodulation. However, the identification of novel, structurally simple peptides and their underlying molecular mechanisms remains to be fully elucidated. In this study, a novel tetrapeptide, designated BSP3 (bursal-derived small peptide 3; Leu-Leu-Pro-Gly, LLPG), with a molecular weight of 398.50 (m/z), was isolated from the bursa of Fabricius of White-feathered broiler chickens and identified by high-performance liquid chromatography (HPLC) and matrix-assisted laser desorption/ionization time-of-flight tandem mass spectrometry (MALDI-TOF-MS/MS); its sequence uniqueness was confirmed by BLASTP analysis. In vivo experiments in mice showed that co-immunization with BSP3 and an inactivated H9 subtype avian influenza virus vaccine significantly enhanced serum antigen-specific IgG responses, promoted IFN-γ and IL-4 secretion, and increased antigen-stimulated splenic lymphocyte proliferation. In vitro , BSP3 promoted the proliferation of the chicken B lymphocyte line DT40 at an optimal concentration of 5 μg/mL. Exploratory transcriptome analysis of BSP3-treated DT40 cells identified 1,100 candidate genes with altered expression, which were annotated to RNA processing, actin crosslink formation, and extracellular matrix organization; MAPK signaling was positioned at the core of the pathway interaction network. RT-qPCR validation was consistent with the transcriptome data. In conclusion, BSP3 is a novel bursa-derived tetrapeptide that shows immunomodulatory activity in mice and in avian B cells. Further validation in avian hosts and functional protection studies are warranted before BSP3 can be considered a candidate vaccine adjuvant.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the immunomodulatory potential of bioactive peptides derived from the bursa of Fabricius in avian species, as these peptides are known to play important roles in immunomodulation.
Design and population
The study involved in vitro experiments using the chicken B lymphocyte line DT40 and in vivo experiments in mice, where BSP3 was co-immunized with an inactivated H9 subtype avian influenza virus vaccine.
Core methods
BSP3 was isolated from the bursa of Fabricius of White-feathered broiler chickens using high-performance liquid chromatography (HPLC) and matrix-assisted laser desorption/ionization time-of-flight tandem mass spectrometry (MALDI-TOF-MS/MS). Its sequence uniqueness was confirmed by BLASTP analysis. In vitro, its effect on B lymphocyte proliferation was tested at an optimal concentration of 5 μg/mL. In vivo, its impact on antigen-specific antibody responses was evaluated through co-immunization with an inactivated H9 subtype avian influenza virus vaccine.
Main results
In vivo experiments showed that co-immunization with BSP3 and an inactivated H9 subtype avian influenza virus vaccine significantly enhanced serum antigen-specific IgG responses, promoted IFN-γ and IL-4 secretion, and increased antigen-stimulated splenic lymphocyte proliferation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted BSP3 as a novel bursa-derived tetrapeptide with immunomodulatory activity in both mice and avian B cells, highlighting its potential as a candidate vaccine adjuvant.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that BSP3 could be a promising candidate for enhancing vaccine efficacy against avian influenza viruses, potentially improving immunological responses in both avian and mammalian hosts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in vivo experiments in mice, and further validation in avian hosts is needed to confirm its potential as a vaccine adjuvant. The evidence is moderate due to the experimental nature of the findings.

禽流感 的新兴技术和人工智能:传播 动力学、种间传播和早期预警

Emerging Technologies and Artificial Intelligence for Avian Influenza: Transmission Dynamics, Interspecies Spread, and Early Warning
作者： Saroj Khatiwada, Stephen Afrifa, Bikash Aryal, Alok Dhakal, Deepa Dhungana, Gourapura J. Renukaradhya, Scott P. Kenney, Ramesh Bahadur Bist
摘要中文翻译禽流感 仍然是一个主要的全球威胁。它会导致反复出现的 暴发、经济损失和公共卫生问题。禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 在野生水禽中自然传播。最近的 暴发 引起了人们对病毒 进化 和跨物种 传播 的担忧。包括奶牛和人类在内的哺乳动物的感染凸显了对更强效 监测 的需求。这篇综合叙述性综述考察了 AIV 流行病学、病毒 进化、宿主 适应和 传播 途径。它特别关注灰尘和气溶胶暴露。它还考虑了 野生鸟类、家禽和哺乳动物之间的相互作用。PubMed 和 Scopus 的结构化检索涵盖了 2015 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月 12 日的文献。选定的 2026 年出版物提供了更多背景信息。该综述综合了科学研究、暴发 调查和流行病学报告。它还研究了传统的诊断和新兴的人工智能 (AI)、分子和基于传感器的技术。当前的 监测 和生物安全方法在跨生产环境的早期检测和协调响应方面面临挑战。支持人工智能的监控和高级诊断可以改善风险评估、预警和 暴发 管理。然而,这些技术需要进一步的现场验证。需要加强环境监测、数据共享和既定生物安全实践的整合。“同一个健康”方法可以将动物、人类和环境联系起来 监测。这种整合可以加强 AIV 的准备并降低未来的 暴发 风险。

Emerging Technologies and Artificial Intelligence for Avian Influenza: Transmission Dynamics, Interspecies Spread, and Early Warning

Authors: Saroj Khatiwada, Stephen Afrifa, Bikash Aryal, Alok Dhakal, Deepa Dhungana, Gourapura J. Renukaradhya, Scott P. Kenney, Ramesh Bahadur Bist
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza remains a major global threat. It causes recurring outbreaks, economic losses, and public health concerns. Avian influenza virus (AIV) circulates naturally in wild waterfowl. Recent outbreaks have raised concerns about viral evolution and cross-species transmission. Infections in mammals, including dairy cattle and humans, highlight the need for stronger surveillance. This integrative narrative review examines AIV epidemiology, viral evolution, host adaptation, and transmission pathways. It gives particular attention to dust and aerosol exposure. It also considers interactions among wild birds, poultry, and mammals. Structured searches of PubMed and Scopus covered the literature from January 2015 to 12 December 2025. Selected 2026 publications provided additional context. The review synthesizes scientific studies, outbreak investigations, and epidemiological reports. It also examines conventional diagnostics and emerging artificial intelligence (AI), molecular, and sensor-based technologies. Current surveillance and biosecurity approaches face challenges in early detection and coordinated response across production settings. AI-enabled monitoring and advanced diagnostics may improve risk assessment, early warning, and outbreak management. However, these technologies require further field validation. Stronger integration of environmental monitoring, data sharing, and established biosecurity practices is needed. A One Health approach can connect animal, human, and environmental surveillance. Such integration may strengthen AIV preparedness and reduce future outbreak risks.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Avian influenza A viruses, avian-origin zoonotic infections, and qualified avian subtypes. Biologically related animal, taxonomic, model, differential-diagnosis and comparative entities are supplementary-only unless material target-specific evidence is also present; only explicit lexical or unrelated hard exclusions are terminal.
Evidence base and method
Structured searches of PubMed and Scopus covered the literature from January 2015 to 12 December 2025. Selected 2026 publications provided additional context. The review synthesizes scientific studies, outbreak investigations, and epidemiological reports.
Consensus and conclusions
Avian influenza virus (AIV) circulates naturally in wild waterfowl. Recent outbreaks have raised concerns about viral evolution and cross-species transmission. Infections in mammals, including dairy cattle and humans, highlight the need for stronger surveillance.
Controversies and gaps
Current surveillance and biosecurity approaches face challenges in early detection and coordinated response across production settings. These technologies require further field validation.
Research and practice implications
AI-enabled monitoring and advanced diagnostics may improve risk assessment, early warning, and outbreak management. Stronger integration of environmental monitoring, data sharing, and established biosecurity practices is needed. A One Health approach can connect animal, human, and environmental surveillance. Such integration may strengthen AIV preparedness and reduce future outbreak risks.

人类甲型流感 (H5N1) 感染的 抗病毒药物 治疗:治疗 时机、临床结果、病毒学结果和 抗病毒药物 耐药性:系统评价

Antiviral Treatment of Human Influenza A(H5N1) Infection: Treatment Timing, Clinical Outcomes, Virological Outcomes, and Antiviral Resistance: A Systematic Review
作者： Haaly Al Mu'aafy Ruslan Musanip, Juniastuti Juniastuti, Kaiser Gerald Handoyo, Rizal Rachbini Mahlevi
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注人类甲型流感(H5N1)感染的 抗病毒药物 抗病毒药物,检查 治疗 时机、临床结果、病毒学结果和 抗病毒药物 耐药性。核心问题是 抗病毒药物 治疗 是否可以改善人类 H5N1 感染的结果以及是否会产生耐药性。
证据基础与方法
从数据库建立到 2026 年 7 月 1 日,对 PubMed、Scopus 和 ScienceDirect 进行了系统检索。使用纽卡斯尔-渥太华量表和 JBI 病例系列清单评估方法学质量。不同研究的临床和方法学特征各不相同;因此,对调查结果进行了叙述性综合。在综合出版物中的研究结果时,考虑了潜在的人群重叠,以避免重复汇总患者数据。
共识与结论
Oseltamivir treatment was associated with a lower hazard of death than no documented anti-influenza antiviral treatment (adjusted hazard ratio 0.51, 95% confidence interval 0.34–0.77). Treatment within 2 days showed lower odds of death, although the overall estimate was not statistically significant (adjusted odds ratio 0.17, 95% confidence interval 0.03–1.04). Among patients without respiratory failure at treatment initiation, earlier treatment was associated with lower mortality (adjusted odds ratio 0.17, 95% confidence interval 0.03–0.89). Overall, oseltamivir, particularly when initiated early and before advanced respiratory failure, was associated with improved clinical outcomes.
争议与缺口
抗病毒药物 有效性的证据受到观察设计、异质 治疗 时间安排和潜在重叠患者群体的限制。因果推断仍然受到混杂、异质性和潜在重叠研究人群的限制。
科研与实践启示
其他 抗病毒药物 的证据有限,并且确定了一个确认的 治疗 出现的 H274Y 神经氨酸酶抗性事件。需要进一步的研究来阐明 抗病毒药物 治疗 的有效性并解决当前证据的局限性。

Antiviral Treatment of Human Influenza A(H5N1) Infection: Treatment Timing, Clinical Outcomes, Virological Outcomes, and Antiviral Resistance: A Systematic Review

Authors: Haaly Al Mu'aafy Ruslan Musanip, Juniastuti Juniastuti, Kaiser Gerald Handoyo, Rizal Rachbini Mahlevi
Original abstract (English)
Human influenza A(H5N1) infection remains a clinically important zoonotic disease, but evidence on antiviral effectiveness is limited by observational designs, heterogeneous treatment timing, and potentially overlapping patient populations. This systematic review synthesized evidence on antiviral regimens, treatment timing, clinical and virological outcomes, and antiviral resistance in laboratory-confirmed human A(H5N1) infection. A systematic search of PubMed, Scopus, and ScienceDirect was conducted from database inception to 1 July 2026. Methodological quality was assessed using the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale and the JBI Case Series Checklist. The clinical and methodological characteristics varied across studies; consequently, the findings were synthesized narratively. Potential population overlap was considered when synthesizing findings across publications to avoid duplicate aggregation of patient data. Of 2,329 records identified, 22 publications were included. Oseltamivir treatment was associated with a lower hazard of death than no documented anti-influenza antiviral treatment (adjusted hazard ratio 0.51, 95% confidence interval 0.34–0.77). Treatment within 2 days showed lower odds of death, although the overall estimate was not statistically significant (adjusted odds ratio 0.17, 95% confidence interval 0.03–1.04). Among patients without respiratory failure at treatment initiation, earlier treatment was associated with lower mortality (adjusted odds ratio 0.17, 95% confidence interval 0.03–0.89). Evidence for other antivirals was limited, and one confirmed treatment-emergent H274Y neuraminidase resistance event was identified. Overall, oseltamivir, particularly when initiated early and before advanced respiratory failure, was associated with improved clinical outcomes, although causal inference remains limited by confounding, heterogeneity, and potentially overlapping study populations.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on antiviral treatment of human influenza A(H5N1) infection, examining treatment timing, clinical outcomes, virological outcomes, and antiviral resistance. The central question is whether antiviral treatment improves outcomes in human H5N1 infection and whether resistance develops.
Evidence base and method
A systematic search of PubMed, Scopus, and ScienceDirect was conducted from database inception to 1 July 2026. Methodological quality was assessed using the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale and the JBI Case Series Checklist. The clinical and methodological characteristics varied across studies; consequently, the findings were synthesized narratively. Potential population overlap was considered when synthesizing findings across publications to avoid duplicate aggregation of patient data.
Consensus and conclusions
Oseltamivir treatment was associated with a lower hazard of death than no documented anti-influenza antiviral treatment (adjusted hazard ratio 0.51, 95% confidence interval 0.34–0.77). Treatment within 2 days showed lower odds of death, although the overall estimate was not statistically significant (adjusted odds ratio 0.17, 95% confidence interval 0.03–1.04). Among patients without respiratory failure at treatment initiation, earlier treatment was associated with lower mortality (adjusted odds ratio 0.17, 95% confidence interval 0.03–0.89). Overall, oseltamivir, particularly when initiated early and before advanced respiratory failure, was associated with improved clinical outcomes.
Controversies and gaps
Evidence on antiviral effectiveness is limited by observational designs, heterogeneous treatment timing, and potentially overlapping patient populations. Causal inference remains limited by confounding, heterogeneity, and potentially overlapping study populations.
Research and practice implications
Evidence for other antivirals was limited, and one confirmed treatment-emergent H274Y neuraminidase resistance event was identified. Further research is needed to clarify the effectiveness of antiviral treatment and to address the limitations in current evidence.

TLR5 信号传导通过 MyD88-JAK1 串扰放大气道上皮细胞对流感的反应

TLR5 signaling amplifies airway epithelial cell responses to influenza through a MyD88–JAK1 crosstalk
作者： Ignacio Caballero, Elaa Chkhaydem, Isabelle Fleurot, I. V. Parfenova, Vanaïque Guillory, Emmanuel Kut, Céline Barc, Jérémy Pezant, Stefanie Ebersberger, Isabelle Seufert

TLR5 signaling amplifies airway epithelial cell responses to influenza through a MyD88–JAK1 crosstalk

Authors: Ignacio Caballero, Elaa Chkhaydem, Isabelle Fleurot, I. V. Parfenova, Vanaïque Guillory, Emmanuel Kut, Céline Barc, Jérémy Pezant, Stefanie Ebersberger, Isabelle Seufert

禽流感 疫苗 能跟上不断变化的病毒吗?

Can a bird flu vaccine keep up with a virus that keeps changing?
新闻简报英国剑桥的一项研究表明,计算机设计的禽流感 疫苗 可以保护小鼠免受三种 H5N1 病毒株的侵害,这表明保护范围更广。由 DIOSynVax 开发的 疫苗 覆盖了多个 H5 分支,并且在小鼠中没有显示出任何疾病迹象。然而,在使用 疫苗 之前,还需要进一步的测试和人体试验。

一项研究表明,计算机设计的禽流感 疫苗 可以保护小鼠免受三种 H5N1 病毒株的侵害,这为人体试验前提供了更广泛保护的早期证据。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Can a bird flu vaccine keep up with a virus that keeps changing?

News Brief
A study in Cambridge, UK, demonstrated that a computer-designed bird flu vaccine protected mice against three H5N1 strains, suggesting broader protection. The vaccine, developed by DIOSynVax, covered multiple H5 branches and showed no signs of illness in mice. However, further testing and human trials are required before the vaccine can be used.
Original source text (English)
Subscribe news videos images take action earthpedia earthsnap Subscribe Bird flu’s continued evolution is driving efforts to develop vaccines that protect against a wider range of strains. Credit: Shutterstock November 10, 2026 Can a bird flu vaccine keep up with a virus that keeps changing? A computer-designed bird flu vaccine protected mice against three H5N1 strains, offering early evidence of broader protection before human trials. ByEric Ralls Founder Follow Earth on Google Governments hold doses of bird flu vaccine in storage for the day the virus starts spreading between people. Each of those vaccines was built around a single virus sample. H5 bird flu, meanwhile, keeps splitting into branches that look different to the immune system. A Cambridge team built a rival vaccine, with active ingredients drawn up by computer instead of copied from one virus. Researchers gave it to mice, then exposed separate groups to three H5N1 viruses spanning two decades of evolution. Every one of those mice lived. Half the mice given the stockpile-style vaccine died after exposure to the clade 1 virus. The study was led by Jonathan Heeney, a professor of research in vaccinology in the Department of Veterinary Medicine at the University of Cambridge. The vaccine comes out of the university spin-out DIOSynVax, where he and several co-authors are employees or shareholders. The tests were in mice and ferrets, and no one has given this vaccine to a person. In an interview with Earth.com, Heeney said vaccine design has spent most of a century looking the wrong way. “For more than 70 years, vaccines against rapidly evolving viruses have largely been designed by looking backwards at the pathogen that already exists,” he said. Stockpiles aim at a virus that moved Bird flu now turns up in wild birds, poultry and mammals on nearly every continent. The H5 family has broken into branches that evolve on their own in Asia, Europe, Africa and the Americas. How dangerous those branches are varies sharply. The clade 2.3.4.4b viruses spreading in the Americas have caused 97 known human cases and three deaths, and most of those infections were mild. These viruses have also spread through dairy herds. The 2.3.2.1e viruses circulating in Southeast Asia have killed more than half the people with reported infections over the past two years. The bird flu vaccines in national stockpiles are matched to single virus samples, picked through the World Health Organization’s influenza program. Those vaccines haven’t been widely tested against the other H5 branches now circulating, the authors wrote. “These vaccines are based on single viral isolates, whereas H5Nx viruses have continued to evolve and diversify into multiple lineages around the world,” Heeney told Earth.com. “In many cases, viral evolution has outpaced the strains on which those stockpiled vaccines were originally based.” An antigen drawn up from a database The part of a vaccine that teaches the immune system has a name: the antigen. Rather than lift one from a single virus, the team pooled the genetic sequences of H5 viruses collected worldwide by surveillance programs. Software then drew up antigens carrying the features shared across the whole group. The vaccine carries mRNA instructions for making three designed antigens. One is based on the outer protein the virus uses to get into a cell, and a second on the protein it uses to get back out. The third is a small protein that helps rouse the immune response. The team screened the candidates in cells, then packaged mRNA encoding the selected antigens inside tiny fat particles, using the same delivery approach as mRNA COVID-19 vaccines. Heeney said the design looks ahead instead of back. He noted that his platform uses computational biology to anticipate viral evolution and design broadly protective vaccine antigens before the next outbreak occurs. Mice survived three different H5N1s The team immunized mice, then exposed them to H5N1 viruses from three branches that have all infected people: clade 1, clade 2.3.2.1e and clade 2.3.4.4b. All mice given the designed vaccine survived exposure to their assigned H5N1 virus, with no weight loss or signs of illness. Four days after exposure, no virus could be recovered from their lungs or spleens. The stockpile-style vaccine matched to 2.3.4.4b protected fully against its own matched virus. Against the 2.3.2.1e virus, 67% of those mice survived, and against clade 1, 50% did. Blood tests pointed the same way. Antibodies from the designed vaccine blocked lab-made stand-ins for eleven H5 branches. Each stockpile-style vaccine covered only its own corner of the family. Ferrets lived without the usual antibody score Ferrets catch and spread flu much as people do, making them an important model for testing influenza vaccines. The team exposed them to a 2.3.4.4b H5N1 taken from an Italian chicken. In the entry protein, it differs from the stockpile vaccine’s strain by four amino acids. Every unvaccinated ferret died or reached the humane endpoint by day eight. So did half of those given the stockpile-style vaccine. All ferrets given the lower dose of the designed vaccine survived without weight loss or fever. One ferret given the higher dose reached the humane endpoint. The oddity sits in the bloodwork. On the day of exposure, those ferrets scored below the antibody level flu researchers treat as protective against that virus. Most survived despite those low antibody readings. Something other than that one measure was carrying the protection, the authors wrote, most likely antibodies against the exit protein working alongside antibodies against the entry protein. Questions before human trials The comparison is not clean, and the authors wrote as much. Their vaccine is mRNA, while the benchmark is a killed whole virus, so dose, formulation and delivery all differ along with the antigen design. Comparing both antigen designs using the same mRNA platform would help isolate the effect of antigen design. The mice also got a single dose of the stockpile-style comparator, a benchmark taken from older work. And the designed vaccine’s edge over the stockpile shots can’t yet be pinned on the antigen design alone. Nobody has measured whether any of this holds in people. The next steps are manufacturing the vaccine to meet regulatory standards and a first trial in humans, Heeney said, and DIOSynVax is raising money to pay for them. The designed antigen sequences are patented. What the virus does in the meantime is the part nobody controls. “It’s when bird flu adapts to live in the airways of mammals that we start to get really nervous,” Heeney said. That is the shift researchers watch for in dairy cows and pigs, and the reason a bird flu vaccine that protects against multiple branches could be valuable. The full study was published in the journal Cell Reports Medicine. —– Like what you read? Subscribe to our newsletter for engaging articles, exclusive content, and the latest updates. H5N1 禽流感危机:工会警告称,美国奶制品工人感染病毒的人数远远多于报道的人数

H5N1 bird flu crisis: Far more US dairy workers infected than reported, union warns
新闻简报美国农场工人联合会 (UFW) 对美国奶制品工人 H5N1 禽流感感染漏报表示担忧,警告称实际病例数远高于官方数据。以华盛顿州亚基马谷为中心的 暴发 导致许多工人因担心被驱逐出境、工资损失和雇主报复而在没有接受检测的情况下患病。症状从轻微到严重不等,包括高烧、剧烈咳嗽和剧烈骨痛。尽管美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 自 2024 年以来已确认 74 例人类病例,但 UFW 坚称对农场的实际影响更大。该工会呼吁改进安全培训并加强对工人的保护,强调需要更好的报告和应对机制来应对日益增长的健康风险。

时间
该文章发表于 2026 年 11 月 12 日。暴发 被描述为正在进行中,最近有华盛顿州亚基马山谷出现疾病和至少一人死亡的报告。
地点与对象
暴发 以华盛顿州的亚基马谷为中心,影响乳制品工人和牧场员工。受影响人口包括农业劳动力,但没有提供具体的人口统计信息。
事件
联合农场工人联合会 (UFW) 警告称,感染 H5N1 禽流感的美国奶制品工人的真实人数明显高于政府官方数据显示的数字。工会强调,由于恐惧和经济压力,许多工人在没有接受检测的情况下就生病了。
规模、影响与风险
UFW 表示,官方病例数严重低估了病毒的传播情况。亚基马山谷至少有 1 人死亡与 暴发 有关。报告的症状从轻微到严重不等,包括高烧、剧烈咳嗽和剧烈骨痛。
应对、状态与不确定性
自 2024 年以来,美国疾病控制与预防中心已在全国范围内确认了 74 例人类病例,但该工会声称实际死亡人数要高得多。由于担心被驱逐出境、工资损失和雇主报复,生病的工人无法寻求医疗帮助或报告症状。工会还呼吁进行适当的安全培训和加强保护。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

H5N1 bird flu crisis: Far more US dairy workers infected than reported, union warns

News Brief
The United Farm Workers (UFW) have raised concerns about the underreporting of H5N1 bird flu infections among US dairy workers, warning that the actual number of cases is much higher than official figures. The outbreak, centered in Washington State’s Yakima Valley, has led to numerous workers falling ill without being tested due to fear of deportation, lost wages, and employer retaliation. Symptoms range from mild to severe, including high fevers, heavy coughs, and intense bone pain. While the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) has confirmed 74 human cases since 2024, the UFW insists the real impact on farms is greater. The union has called for improved safety training and stronger protections for workers, emphasizing the need for better reporting and response mechanisms to address the growing health risk.
Original source text (English)
Home News Royal Entertainment Trending World Sports Sci-Tech Health H5N1 bird flu crisis: Far more US dairy workers infected than reported, union warns Fear of deportation and lost wages keeps sick workers untested By Najam-ul-Saqib | Published November 12, 2026 Make us preferred on Google H5N1 bird flu crisis: Far more US dairy workers infected than reported, union warns The true number of cattle ranch and dairy workers infected with H5N1 bird flu in the United States is significantly higher than official government figures show, according to a stark new warning from the United Farm Workers (UFW). A hidden outbreak in Washington Speaking out about an active outbreak in Washington State’s Yakima Valley, union representatives revealed that numerous dairy workers are falling ill without ever being tested. Based on direct worker testimonies, the UFW stated that official case counts severely underestimate the spread of the virus, which has recently been linked to at least one fatality in the region. While the US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention has officially confirmed 74 human cases nationwide since 2024, advocates insist the real toll on farms is much larger. Fear and financial pressure Several barriers are stopping sick workers from seeking medical help or reporting symptoms. The union noted that an ongoing surge in immigration enforcement in the Yakima Valley has left many agricultural laborers terrified of interacting with public health agencies. Additionally, a lack of paid sick leave and fear of employer retaliation force many laborers to keep working while sick, allowing milk production to continue uninterrupted. Severe symptoms and lack of training Workers have reported symptoms ranging from mild respiratory issues and pinkeye to severe flu-like illness involving high fevers, heavy coughs, and intense bone pain. Although most farms provide basic protective gear like goggles, the union warned that workers urgently need proper safety training and stronger protections as the virus continues spreading to new states. 研究确定了在老挝传播的 12 种 禽流感 基因型 - Bioengineer.org

Study Identifies Twelve Avian Influenza Genotypes Circulating Across Laos - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报Bioengineer.org 于 2026 年 11 月 12 日发表的一项研究确定了 2019 年至 2024 年间在老挝传播的 基因组 病毒的 12 种不同基因型。该研究涉及对 37 种 H5 高致病性 基因组 病毒进行全 基因组 测序和系统发育分析,揭示了持续的病毒循环、广泛的遗传多样性以及跨多个分支的持续重配。进化枝 2.3.2.1e H5N1 病毒占主导地位,通过与进化枝 2.3.4.4b H5N1 和欧亚低致病性 禽流感 病毒重配产生新的基因型。2022 年至 2024 年持续检测到 E7 基因型,与柬埔寨和越南的人类和哺乳动物感染有关。该研究还指出,零星发现了进化枝 2.3.4.4b 和 2.3.4.4h 病毒。研究人员强调了 基因组监测 对监测东南亚跨境 传播 和人畜共患病风险的重要性。研究结果不包括具体的公共卫生应对措施或干预措施,但强调了持续监测的必要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究分析了2019年至2024年间在老挝分离的H5高致病性禽流感病毒。该新闻报道的发表日期为2026年11月12日。
地点与对象
该研究重点关注老挝,还比较了柬埔寨和越南的基因组数据。受影响的人群包括家禽和潜在的人类患者,因为一些基因型与人类感染有关。
事件
一项研究确定了 2019 年至 2024 年间在老挝传播的 禽流感 病毒的 12 种不同基因型。Bioengineer.org 于 2026 年 11 月 12 日报道了该研究结果,并且基于基因组测序和系统发育分析。
规模、影响与风险
该研究发现持续的病毒循环、广泛的遗传多样性以及跨多个进化枝和基因型的持续重配。2022年至2024年连续检测到E7基因型,部分基因型与人畜共患和跨物种风险相关。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究强调需要持续的 基因组监测 来跟踪跨境 传播 和人畜共患风险。这些发现基于基因组数据和系统发育分析,没有明确提及当前的公共卫生反应或干预措施。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Study Identifies Twelve Avian Influenza Genotypes Circulating Across Laos - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A study published on November 12, 2026, by Bioengineer.org identified twelve distinct genotypes of avian influenza viruses circulating in Laos between 2019 and 2024. The research involved whole-genome sequencing of 37 H5 high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses and phylogenetic analysis, revealing persistent viral circulation, extensive genetic diversity, and ongoing reassortment across multiple clades. Clade 2.3.2.1e H5N1 viruses predominated, generating novel genotypes through reassortment with clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 and Eurasian low-pathogenicity avian influenza viruses. Genotype E7 was continuously detected from 2022 to 2024 and was associated with human and mammalian infections in Cambodia and Vietnam. The study also noted that clade 2.3.4.4b and 2.3.4.4h viruses were identified sporadically. The researchers emphasized the importance of genomic surveillance to monitor transboundary transmission and zoonotic risks in Southeast Asia. The findings do not include specific public health responses or interventions but highlight the need for ongoing monitoring.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Friday, November 13, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Study Identifies Twelve Avian Influenza Genotypes Circulating Across Laos by November 12, 2026 in Biology, Health Reading Time: 2 mins read 0 Study Identifies Twelve Avian Influenza Genotypes Circulating Across Laos Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Whole-genome sequencing of 37 H5 high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses isolated in Laos between 2019 and 2024 has revealed persistent viral circulation, extensive genetic diversity, and ongoing reassortment across multiple clades and genotypes. High pathogenicity avian influenza viruses belonging to several H5 clades have caused outbreaks in Laos since 2004, with clade 2.3.2.1e H5N1 viruses predominating in the country since 2015. Despite these recurring outbreaks, genomic data on the H5 lineages circulating within Laos after 2018 remained limited. To investigate the genetic characteristics of these circulating pathogens, researchers sequenced 37 isolates collected from 2019 to 2024 and evaluated them using phylogenetic analyses alongside publicly available Lao avian influenza sequences gathered between 2015 and 2024. Discover more Engineering job board Research publication platform Scientific instrument sales Laboratory equipment supplier Learn Engineering Based on gene constellations, the investigators identified 12 distinct genotypes spanning three hemagglutinin clades. These comprised seven genotypes within clade 2.3.2.1e designated E1 through E7, three genotypes within clade 2.3.4.4b designated B1 through B3, and two genotypes within clade 2.3.4.4h designated H1 and H2. Clade 2.3.2.1e H5N1 viruses predominated overall during the study window, generating novel genotypes through reassortment events involving clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 and Eurasian low-pathogenicity avian influenza viruses. In contrast to this dominant lineage, clade 2.3.4.4b H5Nx and clade 2.3.4.4h H5N6 viruses were identified only sporadically. Phylogenetic comparisons demonstrated that genetically related clade 2.3.2.1e H5N1 genotypes co-circulated across Laos, Cambodia, and Vietnam, while clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 strains identified in Laos in 2023 clustered with viruses documented in East Asia and North America since 2021. The analysis also highlighted zoonotic and cross-species concerns among specific lineages. Genotype H2, representing a clade 2.3.4.4h H5N6 virus, was genetically similar to H5N6 strains epidemiologically linked to a human infection in Laos and detected in both poultry and human patients in China. Discover more Track Biotech Tour Colleges Donate to Research Find Study Aids Bioengineering consulting services Additionally, genotype E7 was detected continuously from 2022 to 2024, exhibiting the identical gene constellation found in viruses associated with human and mammalian infections in Cambodia and Vietnam. The researchers noted that these findings underscore the persistent circulation and reassortment of H5 viruses in Laos, demonstrating the need for sustained genomic surveillance to track transboundary transmission and zoonotic risks across Southeast Asia. The study found persistent viral circulation, extensive genetic diversity, and ongoing reassortment across multiple clades and genotypes. Genotype E7 was continuously detected from 2022 to 2024, and some genotypes were associated with zoonotic and cross-species risks.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study highlights the need for sustained genomic surveillance to track transboundary transmission and zoonotic risks. The findings are based on genomic data and phylogenetic analysis, with no explicit mention of current public health responses or interventions.
原文 / Source

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

人类可能面临严重的禽流感问题

Humans Might Have a Serious Bird Flu Problem on Their Hands
新闻简报A resident in Yakima County, Washington State, died from bird flu (H5N1) earlier this week, marking a concerning development in the spread of the virus. H5N1, a highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI), has been impacting avian populations for decades and recently affected the dairy industry. In 2022, hundreds of millions of birds, both wild and domestic, died due to the virus. A 2024 study found H5N1 had reached South America, infecting and killing thousands of elephant seals. The virus has also been detected in mink farms in Utah, where its cellular structure closely resembles that of humans, raising fears of potential adaptation. While the CDC stated the virus contained no changes making it more contagious, virologists are concerned about its ability to evolve. In May 2025, the U.S. Secretary of Health canceled a $700 million contract with Moderna to develop an mRNA vaccine for H5N1, highlighting concerns about preparedness. Officials in Finland and Spain took swift action by culling infected mink and fox populations, but U.S. authorities have been less proactive, prompting warnings about the risks of human infection.
查看新闻五要素
时间
本周早些时候,在 2026 年和 2022 年,进行了 2024 年研究和 2025 年活动。
地点与对象
亚基马县、华盛顿州和整个美国,其中提到了南美洲和阿根廷的象海豹。
事件
华盛顿州亚基马县的一名居民死于禽流感 (H5N1),H5N1 病毒已传播到多种动物物种,包括牛、象海豹和水貂,引发了人们对人类潜在适应性的担忧。
规模、影响与风险
2022年,数亿只鸟类死亡,南美洲数千只象海豹被感染并死亡,犹他州的水貂养殖场发现了H5N1病毒。疾病预防控制中心指出,没有任何变化使其更具传染性,但病毒学家担心该病毒会适应人类。
应对、状态与不确定性
芬兰和西班牙的官员扑杀了受感染的水貂和狐狸,但美国官员却没有采取任何措施来遏制传播。2025 年取消 H5N1 疫苗 合同的决定引发了人们对准备情况的担忧。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Humans Might Have a Serious Bird Flu Problem on Their Hands

News Brief
A resident in Yakima County, Washington State, died from bird flu (H5N1) earlier this week, marking a concerning development in the spread of the virus. H5N1, a highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI), has been impacting avian populations for decades and recently affected the dairy industry. In 2022, hundreds of millions of birds, both wild and domestic, died due to the virus. A 2024 study found H5N1 had reached South America, infecting and killing thousands of elephant seals. The virus has also been detected in mink farms in Utah, where its cellular structure closely resembles that of humans, raising fears of potential adaptation. While the CDC stated the virus contained no changes making it more contagious, virologists are concerned about its ability to evolve. In May 2025, the U.S. Secretary of Health canceled a $700 million contract with Moderna to develop an mRNA vaccine for H5N1, highlighting concerns about preparedness. Officials in Finland and Spain took swift action by culling infected mink and fox populations, but U.S. authorities have been less proactive, prompting warnings about the risks of human infection.
Original source text (English)
Gear-obsessed editors choose every product we review. We may earn commission if you buy from a link. Why Trust Us? Earlier this week, a resident in Yakima County in Washington State died from bird flu , a highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) virus. Known in more scientific circles as the H5N1, the virus isn’t nearly as threatening to humans as the Covid-19 coronavirus (whose first victims were similarly located in Washington State back in February 2020). But some experts are growing increasingly concerned that this influenza, which for decades has decimated avian populations (and in more recent years, severely impacted the dairy industry ), could develop into something more sinister. The first known case of H5N1 first appeared in 1996 in flocks of domestic waterfowl in southern China. By its nature as an HPAI virus, H5N1 is tailored to latch on to avian cells rather than mammalian cells, including a human’s. However, things have slowly been changing over the decades, and H5N1 mutations have made the virus much more adept at attacking mammals . The newest threat originated during the depths of the Covid-19 pandemic as H5N1 wreaked havoc on seabirds in Europe . The virus eventually found its way to the U.S. in 2022, and hundreds of millions of birds , both wild and domestic, died as a result. A 2024 study by UC Davis reported that the virus reached the very tip of South America, infecting and killing thousands of elephant seals and leaving their bodies strewn along Argentinian beaches . Of course, elephant seals are famously not birds, and during that same time, bovine populations in the U.S. started exhibiting signs of H5N1 infections, driven by polymerase mutations that allowed for better replication in bovine cells . Concerningly, a 2024 study demonstrated that just a single mutation to the bovine influenza H5N1, specifically the hemagglutinin (the surface protein that drives attachment), could switch the virus’s specificity to target humans. Intermediate species can help the virus make the jump to humans (one theory suggests that this is how Covid-19 spread from bats to humans), and last month ProPublica reported that a recent outbreak of H5N1 at a mink farm in Utah is particularly distressing. Unlike those of cows or elephant seals, mink lungs and airways closely resemble ours at a cellular level, raising fears that the virus could adapt to infect humans more easily. When H5N1 impacted mink and fox farms in Finland and Spain , officials moved to cull the infected populations, killing tens of thousands of animals in the process. But ProPublica says state and federal officials in the U.S. have instead done little to curtail the spread. “Every other country that I’m aware of that has had H5N1 in fur farms, depopulation has happened pretty quickly,” avian influenza researcher Tom Peacock told ProPublica. “The fact that the first instinct wasn’t just to depopulate and figure stuff out later is very concerning to me.” If the worst comes to pass, the Trump Administration has gone out of its way so that the country—and the rest of the world—will be vulnerable to the virus. In May 2025, Robert F. Kennedy, the U.S. Secretary of Health of Human Service and well-known vaccine skeptic, cancelled a $700 million contract with Moderna to develop an mRNA vaccine for H5N1. The tragic death earlier this month came from the H5N1 B.13 genotype variant seen in cows, and a CDC spokesperson speaking with Associated Press said that the virus contained no changes that would make it more contagious—but the threat very much remains. “A bad scenario would be that someone working with cattle brought the ‘regular’ human flu in, got [H5N1] on top of that, and that develops into a mix that can spread but to which people have less immunity,” public health virologist Marion Koopmans told The Telegraph . “The chances of such an event are very low, but it is a concern shared among virologists.” Darren lives in Portland, has a cat, and writes/edits about sci-fi and how our world works. You can find his previous stuff at Gizmodo and Paste if you look hard enough. The Lost Ark of the Covenant May Not Be Lost Could Amelia Earhart Have Eaten the Giant Crabs? Scientists Probed the ‘Mad Genius’ Myth A New Clue in the Search for Noah’s Ark Scientists Found the Secret to Happiness A Scientist Solved a Roman Cup’s Magic Trick

Earlier this week, in 2026, and in 2022, with a 2024 study and a 2025 event.

Yakima County, Washington State, and the U.S. in general, with mention of South America and elephant seals in Argentina.
Event
A resident in Yakima County, Washington State, died from bird flu (H5N1), and H5N1 has been spreading to various animal species, including cows, elephant seals, and mink, raising concerns about potential human adaptation.
Scale, impact and risk
Hundreds of millions of birds died in 2022, thousands of elephant seals were infected and killed in South America, and H5N1 has been found in mink farms in Utah. The CDC noted no changes making it more contagious, but virologists are concerned about the virus adapting to humans.
Response, status and uncertainty
Officials in Finland and Spain culled infected mink and fox populations, but U.S. officials have done little to curtail the spread. A 2025 decision to cancel a vaccine contract for H5N1 has raised concerns about preparedness.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

科学家警告说,禽流感病毒通过自然混合获得功能

Bird flu viruses gaining function through natural mixing, warn scientists
新闻简报最近,禽流感 A (H5N1) 在柬埔寨的死灰复燃引起了科学家的担忧。2023 年 2 月报告了第一例人类病例,一名 11 岁儿童死于严重肺炎。此后,又有39人被感染,15人死亡,病死率约为40%。研究人员认为,该病毒可能通过重配获得了新的遗传物质,从而可能增加其感染人类的​​能力。虽然没有证据表明 H5N1 已经进化到可以在人类之间传播,但重组过程被视为一个警告信号。在全球范围内,H5N1 病毒已感染了数百万只鸟类,其中包括在美国,该病毒已传播至奶牛并导致死亡。科学家强调需要改进 监测 来追踪基因变化及其对公共健康的影响。作为回应,英格兰和苏格兰已经实施了生物安全措施,美国正在密切关注事态发展。
查看新闻五要素
时间
禽流感病毒于 2023 年 2 月从一名 11 岁儿童开始卷土重来,此后柬埔寨又有 39 人感染,15 人死亡。这篇发表在《新发传染病》杂志上的论文于 2026 年发表,美国上个月出现一名养牛工人死于 H5N1 病毒。英格兰和苏格兰本周发布了“封城令”。
地点与对象
这些事件发生在柬埔寨,特别是金边,涉及普通民众,特别是那些接触病禽的人。美国也受到了影响,奶牛出现病例,最近还出现了一名养牛工人的死亡。本文讨论了全球对 H5N1 进化 的担忧。
事件
据报道,柬埔寨再次出现 禽流感 A (H5N1) 人类病例,首例病例涉及一名 11 岁儿童,死于严重肺炎。科学家认为,该病毒可能通过重配获得了新的遗传物质,从而可能增加其感染人类的​​能力。美国也出现了牛感染 H5N1 病毒的情况,最近有一名农场工人死亡。
规模、影响与风险
柬埔寨已有39人感染,15人死亡,病死率约为40%。该病毒已感染全球数百万只鸟类,包括在美国,病毒已传播到奶牛并导致死亡。科学家警告说,重组可能会导致人畜共患风险发生不可预测的变化,而 H5N1 仍然是新的全球 暴发 的首要关注点。
应对、状态与不确定性
研究人员正在密切关注 H5N1 的 进化,《新发传染病》中的论文强调重配是一个关键因素。科学家尚未证明重配本身使病毒对人类更具传染性,但他们敦促更好的 监测 来追踪变化及其影响。英格兰和苏格兰已经实施了生物安全措施,美国正在监测奶制品工人中的传播情况。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Bird flu viruses gaining function through natural mixing, warn scientists

News Brief
A recent resurgence of avian influenza A (H5N1) in Cambodia has raised concerns among scientists. The first human case was reported in February 2023, involving an 11-year-old who died from severe pneumonia. Since then, 39 more people have been infected, with 15 fatalities, indicating a case fatality rate of around 40%. Researchers suggest that the virus may have gained new genetic material through reassortment, potentially increasing its ability to infect humans. While there is no evidence that H5N1 has evolved to spread between humans, the process of reassortment is seen as a warning sign. Globally, H5N1 has infected millions of birds, including in the US, where it has spread to dairy cattle and caused a death. Scientists emphasize the need for improved surveillance to track genetic changes and their implications for public health. In response, England and Scotland have implemented biosecurity measures, and the US is monitoring the situation closely.
Original source text (English)
The bird flu virus' resurgence began with an 11-year-old. In February 2023, the schoolgirl was admitted to a hospital in Phnom Penh with severe pneumonia – within a day, she was dead. Tests soon revealed that she had contracted avian influenza: Cambodia hadn't reported a human case of bird flu since 2014, but the hiatus was over . Since then, 39 more people have been infected and 15 have died in the Southeast Asian country – a case fatality rate of roughly 40 per cent. Researchers have been scratching their heads as to why, after nine years, this particular stain of H5N1 suddenly started jumping from birds to people again . The strain or clade called 2.3.2.1 had been circulating for almost a decade without spilling into humans. What changed? Now, a paper in Emerging Infectious Diseases offers clues. Scientists say the apparent rise in human cases has coincided with the virus gaining new genetic material and, potentially, new powers of infection, from a second flu virus via a process known as "reassortment". H5N1 has infected millions of wild and domestic birds in recent years - AFP/OSCAR DEL POZO The process is similar to mutation in its impact but technically different. While a mutation arises randomly within the DNA of a single strain of flu, reassortment occurs when the material from two different strains jumble to create a new strain. It's as if the two strains combine through a "very basic form of sex", said Prof Ed Hutchinson, a molecular and cellular virologist at the University of Glasgow. "The two parent viruses ... produce a 'reassortant' progeny virus, which has some genes from one parent and some from the other." Whatever the mechanism, the bird flu virus circulating in Cambodia has changed – and humans who come into contact with sick birds are dying again. There is no evidence that the virus has evolved to spread between humans, but scientists think it may have become more efficient at jumping from birds to people. "What's alarming here is that recent human cases of H5N1 in Southeast Asia appear to come from influenza viruses that have mixed and matched genes," said Prof Hutchinson. "This suggests that the virus, which we thought had been relatively genetically stable in this region, may be undergoing major genetic changes with unpredictable consequences," he told The Telegraph . Dr Erik Karlsson, head of the virology unit at the Pasteur Institute of Cambodia, and co-author of the paper, added: "We've seen a resurgence of reported human H5N1 infections... and the recent cases involved reassortant viruses. That's a clear association, but we haven't demonstrated that reassortment itself made these viruses better at infecting people." Across the globe, scientists are monitoring the evolution of avian flu viruses with mounting concern. It was a bird flu virus that sparked the devastating 1918 Spanish Flu pandemic and they remain the number one risk for a new global outbreak. While in Asia attention is focused on the evolution of clade 2.3.2.1, elsewhere concern centers on clade 2.3.4.4b – the strain of bird flu that has spread aggressively through birds and mammals on every continent. It too appears to be gaining function through reassortment. Since first breaking out in 2020, it has lapped the globe, killing millions of wild and farmed birds, jumping into mammals including sea lions and mink, and is now established in US dairy cattle. A cattle worker in the US died of the disease last month and the farm worker union is warning that it is circulating among employees of dairy farms . This week, England and Scotland issued another 'flockdown', forcing farmers to bring their flocks indoors and instituting other biosecurity measures to prevent contagion. Cattle across the US have been infected with H5N1 - Bloomberg/Shannon VanRaes Reassortment "is more than an epidemiological footnote; it is a warning," the researchers warn in the new paper. Talking specifically about the rejuvenated Cambodian strain, they add: "Such events illustrate how reassortment within long-overlooked ... H5N1 lineages is reshaping zoonotic risk". The process is likely driven by "intensifying poultry production, shifting wild bird migration patterns, and intensively shared human–animal interfaces," they add. "Unless surveillance is reimagined... the next warning might not appear in a dataset or sequence repository, but instead as an outbreak already beyond our control". Professor Prasert Auewarakul, a virologist at Mahidol University in Bangkok, said the findings on reassortment were worrying. "The fact that this virus is a reassortant between two very successful viruses from two continents, one with a probable increased ability to infect humans and the other with a very wide spread in various species, is by itself frightening." Dr Jennifer Nuzzo, director of the Pandemic Center at Brown University, agreed. "Every time H5N1 infects a person, it raises two different questions. How did the virus get from an animal into that person? And could it eventually get better at spreading from person to person? "Sustained spread between people is the change that would raise the prospect of a pandemic. We don't know whether H5N1 will acquire that ability, and I think some of the confidence that it won't is based more on hope than on evidence. "We need to get better at tracking how these viruses change and figuring out what those changes mean," she said. Time
The bird flu virus' resurgence began with an 11-year-old in February 2023, and since then, 39 more people have been infected and 15 have died in Cambodia. The paper in Emerging Infectious Diseases was published in 2026, and the US has seen a cattle worker die of H5N1 last month. England and Scotland issued a 'flockdown' this week.
Location and population
The events occurred in Cambodia, specifically in Phnom Penh, and involved the general population, particularly those in contact with sick birds. The US has also been affected, with cases in dairy cattle and a recent death of a cattle worker. The paper discusses global concerns about H5N1 evolution.
Event
A resurgence of human cases of avian influenza A (H5N1) in Cambodia was reported, with the first case involving an 11-year-old who died from severe pneumonia. Scientists suggest that the virus may have gained new genetic material through reassortment, potentially increasing its ability to infect humans. The US has also seen H5N1 infections in cattle, with a recent death of a farm worker.
Scale, impact and risk
In Cambodia, 39 people have been infected and 15 have died, resulting in a case fatality rate of about 40%. The virus has infected millions of birds globally, including in the US, where it has spread to dairy cattle and caused a death. Scientists warn that reassortment could lead to unpredictable changes in zoonotic risk, and H5N1 remains a top concern for a new global outbreak.
Response, status and uncertainty
Researchers are monitoring the evolution of H5N1 with concern, and the paper in Emerging Infectious Diseases highlights reassortment as a critical factor. Scientists have not demonstrated that reassortment itself made the virus more infectious to humans, but they are urging better surveillance to track changes and their implications. Biosecurity measures have been implemented in England and Scotland, and the US is monitoring the spread among dairy workers.
